Module 2 · Full Build · Data Schema

### GenerativeWarm-Ups

Student-specific cognitive entry points generated overnight from lesson context and individual learning history. Scored nightly by AI against a 10-point rubric.

4 New Tabs
3 New Scripts
2 Studio Flows
10-Point Scoring Rubric
Depends on M1 + M2 Lightweight

What this builds

#### The system in plain language

Each evening, the teacher submits a lesson context for the next class. At 3am, student profiles are updated from evaluation history and competency logs. At 6am on class days, Studio Flow 3 generates one personalized warm-up doc per student and delivers it to their Drive. Students arrive to a prompt shaped by their own learning history. Class discussion follows. That night, the 3am cron evaluates their responses against a 10-point rubric and writes brief feedback back into each doc.

Reads from M1

###### Ledger · ReviewQueue · STAGING_PIPELINE

- Student roster per teacher/period

- Evaluation outcomes and timestamps

- Assignment feedback history

Reads from M2 Lightweight

###### LessonContext · AlignmentLog · CompetencyRegistry

- Tomorrow's lesson objective and vocabulary

- Competencies addressed per lesson

- Full competency text for Flow 3 context

Writes new

###### StudentProfiles · WarmUpQueue · WarmUpRegistry · ClassSchedule

- Aggregated student learning signals

- Per-student pipeline state per lesson

- Doc registry for all warm-up artifacts

- Block schedule day-type configuration

◈
Block schedule awareness: Classes meet on odd or even calendar days (derived from the date's parity), except first period which meets daily. Period day types are configured once in the ClassSchedule tab during setup. The 3am cron checks tomorrow's date parity, identifies which periods meet, and only builds WarmUpQueue rows for classes that have a LessonContext submitted for that date.

Evaluation rubric

#### 10-point scoring system

Word count is computed deterministically by Script 25 before Flow 4 runs. Grammar and engagement are evaluated by Flow 4. The AI never counts words — it only judges quality.

Component |
Criteria |
Points |
Judge |

Word Count — 6 pts maximum |

30+ words |
Meets full threshold |
6 |
Script 25 |

25–29 words |
Near threshold — partial credit |
5 |
Script 25 |

15–24 words |
Minimal attempt |
3 |
Script 25 |

Under 15 words |
Insufficient |
0 |
Script 25 |

Grammar & Sentence Structure — 1 pt maximum |

Meets standard |
Cohesive sentences, no significant errors |
1 |
Flow 4 |

Does not meet |
Significant errors impede comprehension |
0 |
Flow 4 |

Engagement — 3 pts maximum |

Genuine |
Directly addresses the prompt with original thought |
3 |
Flow 4 |

Surface |
On-topic but formulaic or thin |
2 |
Flow 4 |

Minimal |
Tangentially related or very thin |
1 |
Flow 4 |

Off-topic / filler |
Does not engage with the prompt |
0 |
Flow 4 |

Extra Credit |

Feedback reply |
Student wrote a reply to Flow 4's feedback before next class |
+1 |
Script 25 |

Maximum per warm-up (without extra credit) |
10 |
|

◈
Extra credit detection: Flow 4 stamps its feedback with a delimiter marker (── FEEDBACK ──) when writing to the warm-up doc. Script 25's 3am run checks for any text written after that marker. If text exists and is at least 10 words, extra_credit = 1. This is stored in WarmUpQueue and added to the student's WarmUpRegistry score. The extra credit reply is also captured as a signal in StudentProfiles — students who consistently engage with feedback receive harder provocations in subsequent warm-ups.

◈
Zero-response handling: If Script 25 finds no text in the response zone (word count = 0), it stamps the doc INCOMPLETE with score 0 and skips Flow 4 entirely. Flow 4 is never called with an empty response. This prevents wasted API calls and avoids Flow 4 generating feedback for a student who simply wasn't present.

Execution schedule

#### Two cron jobs, four stages

The 3am job runs three stages in sequence. The 6am job is Studio Flow 3. Together they form a pipeline that completes before students arrive.

// ── 3AM NIGHTLY CRON ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Runs every night. Three stages execute in sequence.

STAGE 1 — Script 23 (StudentProfileManager)
→ reads Ledger for all students (email, name, google_id, period)
→ reads AlignmentLog for competency coverage per student
→ reads STAGING_PIPELINE + ReviewQueue for evaluation outcomes
→ reads previous WarmUpQueue rows for warm-up score history
→ detects extra credit replies in warm-up docs (text after FEEDBACK stamp)
→ upserts one row per student in StudentProfiles
structured analytic indicators + one-sentence feedback note per eval
competency coverage map, warm-up score history, extra credit count

STAGE 2 — Script 25 (WarmUpWriter — evaluation job)
→ scans WarmUpRegistry for yesterday's rows where score IS NULL
→ for each: opens warm-up doc, extracts RESPONSE zone text
→ counts words deterministically → computes word count score (0/3/5/6)
→ if word count = 0: stamp INCOMPLETE, score = 0, skip Flow 4
→ if word count > 0: write word count score, set status PENDING_EVAL
→ Studio Flow 4 picks up PENDING_EVAL rows
evaluates grammar (0/1) + engagement (0/1/2/3)
writes { grammar, engagement, feedback } to WarmUpQueue row
→ Script 25 receives result, computes total score
→ writes score to WarmUpQueue + WarmUpRegistry
→ writes brief feedback into warm-up doc below student response
→ marks WarmUpQueue row SCORED

STAGE 3 — Script 24 (WarmUpBridge — queue builder)
→ determines tomorrow's date → checks parity (ODD / EVEN)
→ reads ClassSchedule for periods matching tomorrow's day type + DAILY
→ reads LessonContext for lessons with lesson_date = tomorrow
skips if no LessonContext found for that period (no class = no warm-up)
→ reads Ledger for enrolled students per teacher + period
→ reads StudentProfiles for each student (fresh from Stage 1)
→ writes one row per student to WarmUpQueue
lesson_context_snapshot (JSON), student_profile_snapshot (JSON)
status: PENDING
→ deduplication: skips if lesson_id + student_email already exists

// ── 6AM CLASS-DAY CRON ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Runs every morning. Studio Flow 3 is the only actor.

Studio Flow 3 (Warm-Up Generation)
→ reads WarmUpQueue for PENDING rows
→ for each student: generates personalized warm-up prompt
reads lesson_context_snapshot + student_profile_snapshot from queue row
generates prompt — topically anchored, student-specifically angled
→ creates Google Doc in student warm-up folder
[Admin Root]/[Subject]/[Teacher]/[Period]/Warm-Ups/[Student Name]/
stamps doc: PROMPT zone + RESPONSE zone (student writes here)
→ shares doc with student as editor
→ marks WarmUpQueue row DELIVERED + writes doc_id
→ Script 25 (post-generation job) registers doc in WarmUpRegistry

◈
Why stages run in sequence: Stage 1 must complete before Stage 3 so that WarmUpQueue rows are built with fresh StudentProfile snapshots. Stage 2 (scoring yesterday's warm-ups) runs between them so the extra credit detection in Stage 1 has the previous night's scored data available. Flow 3 runs at 6am rather than 3am to ensure profile snapshots are as current as possible and to minimize the window where a doc sits in a student's Drive before class.

Data model

#### Four new tabs

All four tabs live on the Central Ledger spreadsheet. ClassSchedule is set once during setup. The other three are operational — written continuously by the pipeline.

##### ClassSchedule

Set once during Module 2 setup · Never written by operational scripts · Read by Script 24

###### 5 columns

One row per class period per teacher

ColumnTypeWriterDescription

teacher_emailStringManual / S28Teacher this schedule row applies to. Matches TEACHER_EMAIL Script Property.

periodStringManual / S28Period identifier. e.g. 1, 2, 3, 4. Must match period_or_class values in LessonContext.

day_typeEnumManual / S28When this period meets. ODD = odd calendar days, EVEN = even calendar days, DAILY = every school day (first period).

course_nameStringManual / S28Full course name. Must match subject in CompetencyRegistry for correct competency filtering.

activeBooleanManualTRUE = active this term. FALSE = retired. Allows carrying forward prior-term rows without deleting them.

day_type enum

DAILY→ Meets every school day. First period always uses this value.

ODD→ Meets when tomorrow.getDate() % 2 === 1.

EVEN→ Meets when tomorrow.getDate() % 2 === 0.

◈
Holiday handling: If the teacher doesn't submit a LessonContext for tomorrow, Script 24 finds no matching lesson_date and writes no WarmUpQueue rows — regardless of what ClassSchedule says. The teacher's lesson context submission is the authoritative signal that class is happening. ClassSchedule only determines which periods to check.

##### StudentProfiles

One row per student · Upserted nightly at 3am by Script 23 · Read by Script 24 for queue snapshots

###### 12 columns

JSON fields serialized as strings — parsed by Script 24 when building queue snapshots

ColumnTypeWriterDescription

── IDENTITY ──

student_emailStringS23Primary key. Google account email. Matches google_id field in Ledger col 1.

student_nameStringS23Display name from Ledger. Denormalized for report readability.

google_idStringS23Google account email (same as student_email in this system). Explicit field for M1 ledger compatibility.

teacher_emailStringS23Teacher this student is assigned to. Used to scope profile reads to the correct teacher's class.

periodStringS23Period identifier from Ledger. Used by Script 24 to match students to the correct class section.

── COMPETENCY HISTORY ──

competencies_addressedJSONS23Array of competency IDs addressed in lessons this student attended. e.g. ["8177-12","8175-5"]. Built from AlignmentLog filtered to this teacher + term.

competency_gapsJSONS23Competency IDs in today's lesson that this student has NOT yet encountered this term. Computed at profile update time as set difference. Flow 3 uses this to angle the warm-up toward unaddressed material.

── EVALUATION SIGNALS ──

evaluation_signalsJSONS23Array of last 3 evaluation outcomes. Each: { assignment_id, date, indicators: { strengths: [], gaps: [] }, note: "one sentence" }. Indicators are structured tags extracted from Flow 2 feedback. See JSON example below.

── WARM-UP HISTORY ──

warmup_scoresJSONS23Array of last 10 warm-up scores. Each: { lesson_id, date, word_count_score, grammar, engagement, extra_credit, total }. Used by Flow 3 to calibrate prompt difficulty.

extra_credit_countIntegerS23Running total of extra credit replies this term. Incremented each time Script 25 detects a reply to Flow 4 feedback.

avg_engagement_scoreFloatS23Rolling average of engagement scores (0–3) across all warm-ups this term. Computed by S23. Flow 3 uses this to calibrate prompt complexity — higher average = harder provocation.

── METADATA ──

last_updatedDateTimeS23Server timestamp of last upsert. Allows detecting stale profiles if the 3am cron fails.

shadow_matrixJSON StringS23Per-student × per-unit archetype confidence model. Keys are pacing guide unit IDs. Tracks archetype_history, best_archetype, within_confidence (0–1), cross_confidence (decayed, 0–1), and trend. Drives archetype selection in Flow 3 when cross_confidence ≥ 0.75. Included in the student_profile_snapshot embedded in WarmUpQueue at 3:30am by Script 24.

unit_currentStringS23Pacing guide unit_id active as of last nightly update. Resolved by Script 29 resolveUnitForDate_(). Included in profile snapshot for unit-scoped shadow matrix lookups.

shadow_archetype_noteString | nullS23Pre-formatted plain-text note computed by getStudentProfileSnapshot_() at 3:30am. Example: "Shadow matrix suggests PARADOX (72% confidence, 5 warm-ups in unit S1-U3, trend: rising)." Null when cross_confidence < 0.75 or no shadow history exists. Read by Flow 3 as {shadow_archetype_note} placeholder in the Mode A system prompt. Computed once at snapshot time — no runtime Sheets reads at generation.

evaluation_signals JSON structure — example

[
{
"assignment_id": "VDOE-ABC123-2026",
"date": "2026-01-14",
"indicators": {
"strengths": ["analysis", "industry_terminology"],
"gaps": ["application", "supporting_evidence"]
},
"note": "Strong conceptual grasp but struggles to apply terminology to real scenarios."
},
// ... up to 3 most recent evaluations
]

warmup_scores JSON structure — example

[
{
"lesson_id": "LES-20260114-A3F2",
"date": "2026-01-14",
"word_count_score": 6,
"grammar": 1,
"engagement": 2,
"extra_credit": 1,
"total": 10
},
// ... up to 10 most recent warm-ups
]

◈
Upsert pattern: Script 23 searches for an existing row by student_email. If found, it updates in place. If not found, it appends. This means the StudentProfiles tab stays at exactly one row per student regardless of how many times the 3am cron runs. The upsert is a targeted range write — not a full row rewrite — to minimize Sheets API quota usage.

##### WarmUpQueue

One row per student per lesson · Pipeline state table · Written by S24, consumed by Flow 3, updated by S25 and Flow 4

###### 16 columns

Snapshot-based — profile data embedded at queue-write time

ColumnTypeWriterDescription

── IDENTITY ──

queue_idStringS24Auto-generated. Format: WUQ-YYYYMMDD-XXXXXX (widened from 4 to 6 hex chars — this ID is a lookup-map key in 25_WarmUpWriter.js, so the original 65,536-value/day collision risk was a real one). Primary key.

lesson_idStringS24Foreign key → LessonContext.lesson_id. Identifies which lesson this warm-up is for.

student_emailStringS24Student's Google account email. Deduplication key with lesson_id.

student_nameStringS24Denormalized from Ledger. Used for doc naming and report display.

google_idStringS24Google account email for Drive sharing (addEditor()).

lesson_dateDateS24Denormalized from LessonContext. Used by Script 25 to find yesterday's rows at evaluation time.

── SNAPSHOTS ──

lesson_context_snapshotJSONS24Serialized lesson context at queue-write time: { objective, activity, vocabulary, prior_connection, competency_ids, competency_texts }. Self-contained — Flow 3 never needs to re-read LessonContext.

student_profile_snapshotJSONS24Serialized StudentProfile at queue-write time. Full profile JSON embedded. Flow 3 reads this directly — never reads StudentProfiles tab. Snapshot isolation: late profile changes don't affect in-flight generation.

── PIPELINE STATE ──

statusEnumS24, Flow 3, S25, Flow 4Pipeline lifecycle. See enum below.

doc_idStringFlow 3Google Drive file ID of the generated warm-up doc. Written by Flow 3 on DELIVERED. Null until then.

doc_urlStringFlow 3Full edit URL. Convenience field for dashboard display and WarmUpRegistry.

── SCORING ──

word_countIntegerS25Actual word count of student's response zone text. Computed by Script 25 at evaluation time.

word_count_scoreIntegerS25Points awarded for word count (0, 3, 5, or 6). Written before Flow 4 is called.

grammar_scoreIntegerFlow 40 or 1. Written by Flow 4 after evaluation.

engagement_scoreIntegerFlow 40, 1, 2, or 3. Written by Flow 4 after evaluation.

extra_creditIntegerS250 or 1. Set by Script 25 at next 3am run if student replied to Flow 4 feedback.

total_scoreIntegerS25Sum of all component scores including extra credit. Written by S25 after Flow 4 returns. Maximum 11 (10 + 1 extra credit).

flow4_feedbackTextFlow 4Brief qualitative feedback written by Flow 4. Also written into the warm-up doc below the student's response.

response_textTextS25Student's response text extracted from warm-up doc by Script 25.

archetypeStringFlow 3Archetype selected by Flow 3 — written back to queue row for shadow matrix tracking in S23. Values: BRIDGE · CONCRETE_SCENARIO · PARADOX · PROVOCATION.

bridge_outputTextFlow 52–3 sentence bridge from student's prior warm-up to today's lesson. Written by Flow 5 before Flow 3. Prepended to warm-up doc by Flow 3 when present. Null for students with no prior history.

status enum — WarmUpQueue

PENDING→ Written by S24 at 3am (directly, for a student with no prior warm-up history), or by Flow 5 once it writes bridge_output for a returning student. Awaiting Flow 3 at 6am.

PENDING_BRIDGE→ Written by S24 at 3am instead of PENDING, only for a student with a prior scored response. Awaiting Flow 5, which writes bridge_output and promotes the row to PENDING when done — this is what actually enforces "Flow 5 before Flow 3," not a compound trigger condition on Flow 5.

IN_PROGRESS→ Flow 3 is currently generating this warm-up.

DELIVERED→ Flow 3 has created and shared the doc. S25 has registered it in WarmUpRegistry.

PENDING_EVAL→ S25 has counted words and computed word_count_score. Awaiting Flow 4.

SCORED→ Flow 4 has returned scores. S25 has written total_score and feedback.

INCOMPLETE→ No response detected (word count = 0). Score = 0. Flow 4 not called.

ERROR→ Any script or Flow failure. See error_notes. Retryable.

##### WarmUpRegistry

One row per warm-up doc · Append-only · Written by Script 25 · Never updated or deleted

◈
Same pattern as ReportRegistry. Every warm-up doc is registered here when Script 25 processes the DELIVERED queue row. Scores are updated in-place (this is the one exception to append-only — score columns are written after Flow 4 returns, not at registration time). The tab is the permanent record for term-end grade calculation.

###### 13 columns

Term-end grade calculation reads directly from this tab

ColumnTypeWriterDescription

warmup_idStringS25Auto-generated. Format: WUP-YYYYMMDD-XXXX. Primary key.

queue_idStringS25Foreign key → WarmUpQueue.queue_id. Enables row-level lookup without scanning the full queue.

lesson_idStringS25Foreign key → LessonContext.lesson_id.

lesson_dateDateS25Denormalized. Used for chronological ordering in grade report.

student_emailStringS25Student identifier. Primary filter for grade report generation.

student_nameStringS25Denormalized for report readability.

teacher_emailStringS25Denormalized from LessonContext. Scopes grade reports to the correct teacher.

doc_idStringS25Google Drive file ID. Stable reference to the warm-up doc.

doc_urlStringS25Full edit URL. Direct link for teacher to open individual warm-up docs.

generated_atDateTimeS25When Script 25 registered this doc (after Flow 3 DELIVERED).

total_scoreIntegerS25Final score 0–11. Written after Flow 4 returns. Null until scored. Term report reads this column.

extra_creditIntegerS250 or 1. Updated at next 3am run after feedback reply detected.

termStringS25Value of CURRENT_TERM Script Property at generation time. Term filter for grade reports.

AI layer

#### Two Studio Flows

Flow 3 generates. Flow 4 evaluates. Neither touches data structures directly — they read from and write to WarmUpQueue rows via the Sheets connector. Scripts handle all tab management.

##### Studio Flow 3 — Warm-Up Generation

Trigger: WarmUpQueue row with status PENDING · Runs at 6am · Per-teacher lane architecture from Module 1

◈
Prompt architecture — four student archetypes: Flow 3 receives the lesson context and student profile snapshot. Based on the student's avg_engagement_score, evaluation_signals, and competency_gaps, it selects one of four prompt angles: (1) Paradox — for students strong conceptually but weak on application; surfaces a genuine contradiction in the subject matter that the lesson resolves. (2) Concrete scenario — for students strong on application but weak conceptually; grounds the abstraction in a real industry situation. (3) Bridge — for students with competency gaps in today's duty area; connects today's lesson to prior work they have covered. (4) Provocation — for students strong across the board; a genuine intellectual challenge that has no easy answer and rewards prior knowledge. The prompt is always 3–5 sentences, never a list, never a fill-in-the-blank.

◈
Doc structure Flow 3 must stamp: The warm-up doc needs two clearly delimited zones so Script 25 can reliably extract the response. Zone 1: ── WARM-UP PROMPT ── ... ── END PROMPT ──. Zone 2: ── YOUR RESPONSE ── (student writes below this line). Script 25 extracts everything after the response delimiter for word count and evaluation. Flow 3 must not put any text after the response delimiter.

##### Studio Flow 4 — Warm-Up Evaluation

Trigger: WarmUpQueue row with status PENDING_EVAL · Runs as part of 3am cron Stage 2

Flow 4 receives three inputs: the original warm-up prompt, the student's response text, and the word count score already computed by Script 25. It evaluates only grammar/structure (0 or 1) and engagement (0, 1, 2, or 3). It must return a structured JSON response — no prose, no markdown.

Flow 4 expected JSON response

{
"grammar": 1, // 0 or 1
"engagement": 2, // 0, 1, 2, or 3
"feedback": "Strong connection to branding concepts but the application to event sponsorship could go deeper. Consider how exclusivity affects perceived value."
// feedback: 1-3 sentences. Written into warm-up doc. Student reads before next class.
// Never reveals the score. Never says "you got X points". Pedagogical, not evaluative in tone.
}

◈
Feedback tone constraint: Flow 4's feedback must never mention points, scores, or grades. It is pedagogical — it advances the student's thinking, not their grade anxiety. The feedback is what the student sees when they open their doc before next class. It should make them want to think more about the topic, not calculate their percentage. The score is stored in the system; the feedback is for the student.

◈
Extra credit reply detection: Flow 4 stamps its feedback with ── FEEDBACK ── at the start and ── END FEEDBACK ── at the end when writing to the doc. Script 25's next 3am run reads the doc and checks for any text after ── END FEEDBACK ──. If 10+ words exist there, extra credit = 1. The student never sees instructions to reply — the invitation is implicit in the feedback quality.

Data relationships

#### How scripts connect to tabs

Every read and write relationship in the warm-up pipeline. No script reads from a tab it doesn't own — all cross-tab data flows through queue row snapshots.

Script 23 reads

###### StudentProfileManager

- Ledger (roster, eval timestamps)

- AlignmentLog (competency history)

- STAGING_PIPELINE (eval outcomes)

- ReviewQueue (eval status)

- WarmUpQueue (score history)

- WarmUp docs (extra credit reply)

Writes

- StudentProfiles (upsert)

Script 24 reads

###### WarmUpBridge

- ClassSchedule (day type)

- LessonContext (tomorrow's lessons)

- Ledger (enrolled students)

- StudentProfiles (snapshots)

- CompetencyRegistry (comp texts)

Writes

- WarmUpQueue (new PENDING rows)

Script 25 reads

###### WarmUpWriter

- WarmUpQueue (DELIVERED + PENDING_EVAL rows)

- WarmUp docs (response text, reply detection)

- LessonContext (teacher_email, term)

Writes

- WarmUpRegistry (registration + scores)

- WarmUpQueue (scores, status)

- LessonContext (warm_up_generated)

- WarmUp docs (feedback stamp)

Flow 3 reads

###### Warm-Up Generation

- WarmUpQueue (PENDING rows)

- lesson_context_snapshot (embedded)

- student_profile_snapshot (embedded)

Writes

- WarmUpQueue (doc_id, status DELIVERED)

- Student Drive folder (new warm-up doc)

Flow 4 reads

###### Warm-Up Evaluation

- WarmUpQueue (PENDING_EVAL rows)

- Warm-up prompt (from queue snapshot)

- Student response text (from queue)

- word_count_score (pre-computed)

Writes

- WarmUpQueue (grammar, engagement, feedback)

- Warm-up doc (feedback stamp)

Term Report reads

###### generateWarmUpReport() in S25

- WarmUpRegistry (all rows this term)

- StudentProfiles (names, context)

- ClassSchedule (total possible warm-ups)

Writes

- Google Doc (grade report)

- ReportRegistry (type: WARMUP_TERM)

- Script Properties (last report pointer)

◈
Snapshot isolation is the key architectural decision. Script 24 embeds the full lesson context and student profile into each WarmUpQueue row at 3am. Flow 3 reads only from the queue row — never from LessonContext or StudentProfiles directly. This means a late profile update or lesson context edit after 3am has no effect on that night's warm-up generation. The snapshot is the contract between the pipeline stages.

Classroom Agency System · Module 2 Full Build · Warm-Up Schema v1.0
Scripts: 23 · 24 · 25 · Flows: 3 · 4 · Tabs: StudentProfiles · WarmUpQueue · WarmUpRegistry · ClassSchedule

PacingGuide v2 — new fields per unit

PacingGuide_CAS_Context_v2.json adds four fields not present in the original JSON. Script 29 must be patched to import these before running importPacingGuide().

Field |
Type |
Description |

chain_node | String | Supply-chain positioning label for the unit (e.g. "Producer / Distributor — how value is monetized") |

esports_connection | String | Esports/gaming industry application of the unit's competencies. Used by Flow 3 for industry-grounded examples. |

vocabulary_with_definitions | Array | Array of {term, definition} objects. More useful to Flow 3 than the flat key_vocabulary string — allows definition-aware scaffolding. |

warmup_anchor (enriched) | String | Now the full teacher-authored prompt from the lesson card, not a compressed summary. Script 29 cache truncates to 200 chars for property storage — full text always read from sheet for Flow 3 payloads. |

CompetencyRubrics — SOL correlations added

Each rubric now carries sol_correlations — a subject-keyed object of SOL codes (e.g. {"English": ["9.C","9.DSR","9.RI"], "Mathematics": ["A.1"]}). 197 of 221 competencies have SOL data. The 24 without are CTSO/WBL tasks, holistically assessed by VDOE design. Also added: definition (official VDOE definition text) and required (R or O). Flow 3 can reference SOL subject areas as personalization signals — a student whose shadow matrix shows low engagement on History & Social Sciences-correlated competencies is a different target than one strong there.

Flow 5 — Bridging Flow (new)

Flow 5 runs at 6am on rows S24 wrote as PENDING_BRIDGE instead of PENDING — S24 only writes that status when a prior scored response actually exists, so the trigger condition is simply "Status = PENDING_BRIDGE," not a compound check against flow5_prior_response inside the snapshot. Temperature 0.4 · Max tokens 150. Generates a 2–3 sentence bridge between the student's most recent scored response and today's lesson using pacing_prior_connection from the pacing guide. Output written to bridge_output (col 20 of WarmUpQueue), then Flow 5's own final step sets Status back to PENDING — this hand-off is what enforces the ordering, not the trigger condition alone. Flow 3 prepends bridge_output when present, and its own trigger condition ("Status = PENDING") never changes. A student with no prior history skips Flow 5 entirely — their row starts at PENDING directly, strictly additive. Full specification in CAS_Flow3_Flow4_Specification.html v1.2.

Updated cron sequence: 3:00am S23 → 3:05am S31 → 3:15am S25 → 3:30am S24 → 6:00am Flow 5 → 6:00am Flow 3 → Flow 4

Classroom Agency System · Module 2 · Documentation v2.0

### LessonIntelligence

Two deployable builds. Lightweight automates competency alignment documentation. Full build adds personalized warm-up generation scored overnight by AI.

LIGHTWEIGHT: PRODUCTION READY
WARM-UPS: SPECIFIED
10 New Tabs
11 New Scripts
4 Studio Flows
221 Competencies · 221 Rubrics · 197 SOL
Shadow Matrix · Pacing Guide
Additive · No M1 Changes

What this module does

#### The prerequisite for instruction

Module 1 automates the prerequisite for student submission. Module 2 automates the prerequisite for instruction — the documentation and alignment work that happens before class, and the cognitive priming that happens as students walk in.

◈
Two independent builds. The lightweight build is deployable today and produces immediate value: automatic competency alignment documentation from lesson context submissions, with a term-end report generated on demand. The full warm-up build layers on top and adds student-specific cognitive entry points generated overnight, scored nightly by AI, and feeding a longitudinal student profile. Either build runs independently. The full build requires the lightweight build to be in place first.

◈
Module independence from Module 1. Module 2 adds tabs and scripts to an existing Module 1 installation. No existing scripts are modified. Module 2 can be disabled by setting M2_ENABLED = false in Script Properties. Module 1 continues running identically.

Build 1 — Production Ready

#### Lightweight: Lesson Framing + Alignment

Teacher submits a lesson context via the Teacher Dashboard modal. System logs competency alignment automatically. Term-end documentation report generated on demand.

// LESSON CONTEXT SUBMISSION — complete lightweight flow

Teacher → clicks "New Lesson" in Teacher Dashboard header
→ modal opens · competency tabs load from CompetencyRegistry
→ two tabs: 8175 Marketing · 8177 Management
→ competencies sorted by task number within each course tab
→ teacher fills required fields · selects competencies · clicks "Log lesson"

Script 07 (Teacher Dashboard web app)
→ submitLessonContext(payload)
→ injects teacherEmail + teacherName from Script Properties
→ calls onLessonContextSubmit_(payload) in Script 22

Script 22 (LessonContextHandler)
→ M2_ENABLED guard · tab availability check
→ validateLessonPayload_(): required fields · date · competency IDs
→ supersedeDuplicates_(): mark SUPERSEDED if same teacher+date+period
→ write row to LessonContext · status: RECEIVED
→ call logAlignmentForLesson_(lessonId) in Script 26

Script 26 (CompetencyAlignmentLog)
→ build CompetencyMap from registry
→ for each competency ID: write one row to AlignmentLog (denormalized)
→ update LessonContext status → ALIGNMENT_LOGGED
→ call generateLessonFrame_(lessonId) in Script 27, only if S26 succeeded

Script 27 (LessonFrameGenerator)
→ look up rubric text for the lesson's competencies (Script 32)
→ build the Lesson Frame Doc · register in ReportRegistry (report_type: LESSON_FRAME)
→ update LessonContext status → FRAME_GENERATED
→ Script 07 receives { success: true, frameDocUrl } → toast + opens the Lesson Frame doc

// SAFETY NET
runAlignmentLogBackfill_ (every 5 min)
→ catches RECEIVED rows where S26 failed silently

// TERM-END REPORT (manual)
generateAlignmentReport() from Script 26
→ Google Doc in teacher folder: competencies by course then duty area
→ ✓ addressed (with lesson dates) · ○ not yet addressed
→ registers in ReportRegistry + writes Script Properties

##### Architectural decisions

-
NO QUEUE
Script 22 calls Script 26 directly. No queue needed — no Studio Flows, no async latency. The entire submission pipeline completes synchronously within one web app request. The 5-minute backfill trigger catches any silent failures.

-
MODAL IN S07
Lesson Context form lives inside the Teacher Dashboard. Same deployment, same URL, teacher identity injected server-side from Script Properties. Competency dropdown filtered by course — two tabs, sorted by task number, discovered dynamically from the registry.

-
APPEND-ONLY LOG
AlignmentLog rows are never updated or deleted. Resubmissions append new rows; the old LessonContext row is SUPERSEDED. The log is an audit record, not a live state table. Reports filter by logged_at for the most recent mapping per lesson.

-
DENORMALIZED
AlignmentLog copies lesson fields at write time. The tab is self-contained — an administrator reading an export sees a complete record without cross-referencing other tabs.

-
PER-COURSE DUPES
Foundation competencies (tasks 1–38) are logged separately per course. 8175-5 and 8177-5 are distinct IDs. Each course's coverage is independently tracked and reported in separate sections of the AlignmentReport. Intentional and settled.

-
MULTI-TEACHER
Each colleague gets their own Central Ledger installation. Script 28 (setup wizard extension) handles cloning. M2_ENABLED defaults to false — explicit opt-in required per installation.

Build 2 — Warm-Up System

#### Full Build: Generative Warm-Ups

Student-specific cognitive entry points generated overnight from lesson context and individual learning history. Scored nightly by AI. Feeds a longitudinal student profile.

// ── 3AM NIGHTLY CRON — three stages in sequence ────────────────────────────

STAGE 1 — 3:00am — Script 23 (StudentProfileManager)
→ reads Ledger + AlignmentLog + STAGING_PIPELINE + WarmUpQueue in bulk
→ for each student: compiles class-level competency history
→ extracts structured analytic indicators from Flow 2 feedback (MAX 50 doc opens)
→ reads warm-up scores + extra credit flags from WarmUpQueue (no doc opens)
→ upserts one row per student in StudentProfiles
→ batch writes new students · targeted range writes for updates

STAGE 2 — 3:15am — Script 25 (WarmUpWriter — evaluation job)
→ scans WarmUpRegistry for yesterday's rows where total_score IS NULL
→ for each: opens warm-up doc ONCE
extracts response text + checks for extra credit reply in same pass
counts words deterministically → word count score (0/3/5/6)
→ if word count = 0: stamp INCOMPLETE · score 0 · skip Flow 4
→ if word count > 0: write response_text + status PENDING_EVAL
→ Studio Flow 4 evaluates grammar (0/1) + engagement (0/1/2/3)
returns { grammar, engagement, feedback } as JSON
→ Script 25 computes total · writes scores to queue + registry + doc

STAGE 3 — 3:30am — Script 24 (WarmUpBridge)
→ determines tomorrow's date → parity check (ODD / EVEN)
→ reads ClassSchedule for periods meeting tomorrow (DAILY + day type)
→ for each matching period with a LessonContext submitted:
reads enrolled students from Ledger
calls getStudentProfileSnapshot_() per student
→ computes competency_gaps against today's lesson on the fly
builds all queue rows in memory
writes ONE batch setValues() call per period (not appendRow)
→ marks LessonContext warm_up_generated = QUEUED

// ── 6AM CLASS-DAY CRON ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Studio Flow 3 (Warm-Up Generation)
→ reads WarmUpQueue PENDING rows
→ selects archetype: PROVOCATION → PARADOX → CONCRETE SCENARIO → BRIDGE
→ generates personalized 3-5 sentence warm-up prompt
→ creates Google Doc in student warm-up folder
[Admin Root] / [Course] / [Teacher] / [Period] / Warm-Ups / [Student] /
folder IDs sourced from Script Properties via snapshot — no hardcoding
→ stamps doc: PROMPT zone · RESPONSE zone
→ shares with student as editor
→ marks queue row DELIVERED
→ Script 25 Job 1 registers doc in WarmUpRegistry (5-min safety trigger)

##### Block schedule awareness

Classes meet on odd or even calendar days (derived from date parity). First period meets daily. Period day types configured once in the ClassSchedule tab. Holiday handling is implicit — if no LessonContext is submitted for tomorrow, no warm-ups are generated regardless of the schedule.

◈
Throughput at 150 students across 4 periods: ~40 students per period. Flow 3 at 40 students × 30s = ~2–4 minutes with Studio parallelism. Script 25 evaluation at 40 doc opens × 2s = ~80 seconds per period batch. All stages complete within the 3am–6am window. Batch writes in Script 24 (single setValues() per period) keep queue-build time under 10 seconds total.

##### Ambient onboarding — shadow matrix

Derived from the KOS probabilistic state matrix pattern. The system quietly learns which warm-up archetype produces the best engagement trajectory for each student at the granularity of each unit — without asking the teacher anything. The shadow matrix lives as a JSON field in the teacher profile row of the StudentProfiles tab, updated nightly by Script 23.

◈
Two confidence layers: Within-unit confidence builds across warm-ups in the current unit (history length / 8 expected warm-ups per 3-week unit). Cross-unit confidence is a decayed weighted average across all prior units using a 0.85 decay factor — recent units weighted more heavily. When cross-unit confidence crosses 0.75 for a given student × archetype combination, Script 25 sends the teacher a batched email digest. The teacher can reply OVERRIDE to keep the default selection; otherwise the system locks to the confident archetype for that student's remaining warm-ups in the unit. At 3:30am, getStudentProfileSnapshot_() in Script 23 computes a pre-formatted shadow_archetype_note string for each student — e.g. "Shadow matrix suggests PARADOX (72% confidence, 5 warm-ups in unit S1-U3, trend: rising)" — and embeds it in the student_profile_snapshot JSON on the WarmUpQueue row. Flow 3 reads it as the {shadow_archetype_note} placeholder in the Mode A system prompt. Null when confidence is below threshold. Computed once at snapshot time — no Sheets reads at generation.

◈
Pacing guide as the unit clock: Script 29 resolves any lesson date to its pacing guide unit by matching against approx_start and approx_end date ranges. The 20-unit pacing guide covers the full academic year. The shadow matrix resets its within-unit confidence at each new unit boundary while retaining cross-unit history. The teacher never tags lessons with unit names — the date is the only input.

◈
Warm-up anchor — two-mode generation: Each pacing guide unit contains a teacher-authored warmup_anchor seed prompt. When present, Flow 3 runs in Mode A: personalizing the anchor for each student rather than generating from scratch. The anchor guarantees pedagogical quality; Flow 3 applies the archetype angle and student personalization. When absent (pacing guide not yet imported), Flow 3 runs in Mode B: generative from the lesson context and competency rubrics alone. Both modes are valid — Mode A produces consistently higher quality.

◈
Competency rubric enrichment — weak point 2 resolved: Each selected competency's rubric (from Script 30) is embedded in the WarmUpQueue lesson context snapshot via addRubricsToSnapshot_(). The rubric includes 1–11 skill questions pre-mapped to the four archetypes via archetype_question_map. Flow 3 receives structured question options for every competency regardless of how minimal the teacher's lesson context submission is. A teacher who submits a one-line objective still gives Flow 3 enough material for a quality prompt — the competency rubric carries the pedagogical substance.

##### Artifact-based competency tracking — Script 31

Script 31 runs at 3:05am — between Script 23 (class-level baseline) and Script 25 (evaluation). It reads STAGING_PIPELINE for COMPLETE assignments, joins to the Teacher Matrix's new competency_ids column, and writes per-student competency coverage to StudentProfiles. The result: competencies_addressed reflects work actually produced, not just lessons attended. The warm-up competency gap computation in Script 24 becomes genuinely per-student.

◈
The artifact evidence model: Completion of a work artifact = demonstrated competency exposure. A student who was present but disengaged has no artifact. A student who was absent but completed makeup work has one. Script 31's merge is strictly additive — it never removes competencies from a student's profile, only adds. The Teacher Matrix needs one new column: competency_ids (comma-separated IDs). Run addCompetencyIdsColumn_() from Script 31 once to add it. Run installArtifactSyncTrigger_() to install the 3:05am trigger.

##### Flow 5 — Bridging Flow

Flow 5 generates a 2–3 sentence connector between a student's most recent warm-up response and today's lesson. It runs before Flow 3 on the same PENDING queue row. Script 24 embeds the student's prior response (flow5_prior_response, capped at 800 chars) in the per-student lesson context snapshot at 3:30am. Flow 5 reads this at 6am and writes a bridge paragraph to bridge_output (WarmUpQueue column 21). Flow 3 prepends it to the warm-up doc when present. Students with no prior history receive no bridge — strictly additive.

◈
Temperature 0.4 — lower than Flow 3's 0.7. Flow 3 generates — it needs variation and creative framing. Flow 5 connects — it needs accuracy. A bridge that misrepresents what the student wrote is worse than no bridge. Lower temperature keeps Flow 5 close to what the student actually expressed. Max tokens: 150. One paragraph. Full specification in CAS_Flow3_Flow4_Specification.html v1.2.

##### Data files — what's in them now

Both data files were significantly enriched during the July 2026 build session using the ten Stage lesson cards and both Canvas Common Cartridges.

◈
CompetencyRubrics.json — now includes SOL correlations. 197 of 221 competencies have Virginia Standards of Learning correlations across four subject areas: English (117 competencies), History and Social Sciences (75), Mathematics (16), Science (12). Data sourced from the official VDOE printable versions for both courses — the 8175 file was uploaded directly; the 8177 file was discovered embedded in the Canvas management cartridge at web_resources/Uploaded Media/8177 Printable Version with Standards Correlations.docx. The 24 without SOL correlations are CTSO/WBL/foundation tasks (8175 tasks 24–38 and equivalents) — no SOL by VDOE design, assessed holistically. Each rubric now also carries the official VDOE definition field alongside the existing demonstration_indicators and skill_questions. Flow 3 can use SOL subject area as an additional personalization signal.

◈
PacingGuide_CAS_Context_v2.json — rebuilt from lesson cards. The original pacing guide JSON was a compressed derivative of the ten Stage lesson cards. v2 restores the full source data: warm-up anchors are the complete teacher-authored prompts (not summaries), vocabulary entries have definitions, prior lesson connections are verbatim from the cards. New fields added per unit: chain_node (supply-chain positioning label), esports_connection (esports/gaming industry application), vocabulary_with_definitions (array of term + definition objects). The lesson card structure was verified against Canvas module task ranges for all 20 units — exact matches confirmed for 8175-66/67 (Stage 4), 8177-85–88 (Stage 4), 8177-39–48 (Stage 1), and 8177-63–66 (Stage 5). Use v2 going forward. Script 29 needs a minor schema patch before import — see next steps.

##### Module 3/4 integration — script number collision

A separately-developed Module 3/4 codebase uses script numbers 29 and 30 for different scripts than this build. This is a hard deployment blocker for multi-module installs.

◈
Script number collision — must resolve before combined deployment.
This build: Script 29 = PacingGuideManager · Script 30 = CompetencyRubricImporter.
Module 3/4 build: Script 29 = StudentContextAggregator · Script 30 = SCRSuggestionEngine.
Resolution: renumber Module 3/4 scripts to 32 and 33 (with 33b for SCRRetryRemediation).
No function name conflicts exist — the collision is file-number only. Update all cross-references in the Module 3/4 addendum files before pasting into the same project.
Master script number map: S07 Dashboard · S16 UnifiedSetup · S19 ClonedSheetConfig · S22 LessonContextHandler · S22b CompetencyRegistryImporter · S23 StudentProfileManager · S24 WarmUpBridge · S25 WarmUpWriter · S26 CompetencyAlignmentLog · S28 Module2Setup · S29 PacingGuideManager · S30 CompetencyRubricImporter · S31 ArtifactCompetencyBridge · S32 StudentContextAggregator · S33 SCRSuggestionEngine · S33b SCRRetryRemediation · S08 TeacherConfirmationStep.

##### Setup wizard — contextual gates

Script 28 implements the Contextual Gates design philosophy throughout. Every setup step verifies its own output before declaring success. Every failure surfaces at the point of action with an exact path forward. No instruction is deferred to a document the teacher won't read. The teacher never types something the system already knows. A "complete" state means verified complete — not "the script ran without errors." This philosophy is documented in full in CAS_ContextualGates_DesignPrinciples.html and applies to every deployment-adjacent flow in the system.

##### Cron health monitoring — weak point 3 resolved

Each cron stage writes a health stamp to Script Properties on completion. The subsequent stage reads that stamp at startup and checks whether it's within the expected window. If a stage ran more than 45 minutes ago, Script 25's _checkCronHealth_() sends one email alert to the teacher (or admin) and sets a flag to prevent repeat alerts for the same stale event.

◈
Health stamps written: M2_STAGE1_LAST_RUN / M2_STAGE1_STATUS by Script 23 · M2_STAGE2_LAST_RUN / M2_STAGE2_STATUS by Script 25 · M2_STAGE3_LAST_RUN / M2_STAGE3_STATUS by Script 24. Health checks are non-fatal — the current stage continues regardless. The alert is the signal, not the gate. A teacher who sees the alert knows to check the execution log and can manually trigger the missed stage from the Script Editor.

Warm-Up Evaluation

#### 10-point scoring rubric

Word count computed deterministically by Script 25 before Flow 4 runs. Grammar and engagement evaluated by Flow 4. The AI never counts words — it only judges quality.

Component | Criteria | Points | Judge |

Word Count — 6 pts maximum |

30+ words | Meets full threshold | 6 | Script 25 |

25–29 words | Near threshold | 5 | Script 25 |

15–24 words | Minimal attempt | 3 | Script 25 |

Under 15 words | Insufficient | 0 | Script 25 |

Grammar & Sentence Structure — 1 pt maximum |

Meets standard | Cohesive sentences, no significant errors | 1 | Flow 4 |

Does not meet | Significant errors impede comprehension | 0 | Flow 4 |

Engagement — 3 pts maximum |

Genuine | Directly addresses the prompt with original thought | 3 | Flow 4 |

Surface | On-topic but formulaic or thin | 2 | Flow 4 |

Minimal | Tangentially related or very thin | 1 | Flow 4 |

Off-topic / filler | Does not engage with the prompt | 0 | Flow 4 |

Extra Credit |

Feedback reply | Student wrote 10+ words after Flow 4 feedback stamp | +1 | Script 25 |

Maximum per warm-up | 11 | |

◈
Flow 4 feedback is pedagogical only — no scores mentioned. Students see 1–3 sentences that advance their thinking about the topic. The score lives in WarmUpQueue and WarmUpRegistry. Extra credit is detected the following night — student replies to feedback after class, Script 25 detects it at the next 3:15am run and updates both the queue row and WarmUpRegistry.

◈
Term-end grade report. Run generateWarmUpReport() from Script 25 manually at end of term. Produces a Google Doc with per-student summary: warm-ups generated, completed, points earned, points possible, percentage. Registered in ReportRegistry (type: WARMUP_TERM). Script Properties updated with M2_LAST_WARMUP_REPORT_* keys.

Codebase

#### Ten scripts across two builds

All scripts bound to the Central Ledger spreadsheet project except Script 07 (standalone web app). Scripts 29 and 30 are import utilities — run once manually, then serve as runtime lookup libraries called by Scripts 23 and 24. Function tags: ENTRY = called by another script or client · MANUAL = run once from Script Editor · EXPORT = called by another script file · HOOK = live but dormant.

##### Lightweight Build

SCRIPT 07 — MODIFIED

##### Teacher Dashboard

Standalone Web App · Execute as: Me · Redeploy existing deployment

Existing dashboard extended with the Lesson Context modal. Competency dropdown auto-discovers courses from the registry by teacher email — two tabbed course panels, competencies sorted by task number. Teacher identity injected server-side. All original dashboard code unchanged.

getCompetencies()
submitLessonContext(payload)
S27: frameDocUrl (live — see 27_LessonFrameGenerator.js)
M3 hook: scaffold spans

##### LessonContextHandler

Central Ledger · Called by Script 07

Validates payload, supersedes duplicate submissions for same teacher+date+period, writes to LessonContext tab, calls Script 26 directly. If Script 26 fails, writes error note and returns success — backfill trigger retries. Contains one-time setup functions for use before Script 28 exists.

Central Ledger · Run once manually

One-time CSV importer. Searches teacher Drive folder first, then all of Drive for CompetencyRegistry.csv. Full RFC 4180 CSV parser. Skips duplicate IDs on re-run. Batch writes all new rows in a single setValues() call.

importCompetencyRegistry()
validateRegistryImport()

Central Ledger · Called by Script 22

Append-only alignment logger. Writes one AlignmentLog row per competency per lesson with denormalized fields. Term-end report groups by course then duty area, registers in ReportRegistry, writes Script Properties. buildCompetencyMap_() for pipeline; buildCourseMap_() for reports — two separate map structures, not redundant.

##### Full Warm-Up Build

SCRIPT 23

##### StudentProfileManager

Central Ledger · 3:00am trigger · 13 functions

Stage 1 of the 3am cron. Reads Ledger, AlignmentLog, STAGING_PIPELINE, and WarmUpQueue in bulk — zero doc opens for extra credit (flags already written by Script 25). Opens up to 50 assignment docs to extract structured analytic indicators from Flow 2 feedback. Upserts StudentProfiles: batch append for new students, targeted range writes for updates. Exports getStudentProfileSnapshot_() for Script 24.

updateAllStudentProfiles()
getStudentProfileSnapshot_()
createWarmUpTabs_()
installWarmUpTriggers_()
validateStudentProfiles()

SCRIPT 24

##### WarmUpBridge

Central Ledger · 3:30am trigger · 13 functions

Stage 3 of the 3am cron. Computes tomorrow's day type from date parity. Reads ClassSchedule for matching periods. For each period with a LessonContext: reads enrolled students, calls Script 23's getStudentProfileSnapshot_() (computes competency gaps on the fly), builds all queue rows in memory, writes ONE batch setValues() per period. Lesson context snapshot embeds admin_root_folder_id, teacher_name, and teacher_email from Script Properties — no hardcoded IDs in Flow 3.

buildWarmUpQueues()
createLessonContextWarmUpColumn_()
validateQueueBuild()

SCRIPT 25

##### WarmUpWriter

Central Ledger · 3:15am trigger + 5-min safety net · 19 functions

Two jobs. Job 1 (5-min trigger): registers Flow 3 DELIVERED docs in WarmUpRegistry, marks LessonContext DELIVERED when all students complete. Job 2 (3:15am): opens each warm-up doc once — extracts response text and checks for extra credit reply in the same pass. Writes response_text to queue so Flow 4 never opens docs. Computes word count score, queues PENDING_EVAL for Flow 4, receives scores, writes feedback into doc, updates registry. Term-end grade report registered in ReportRegistry as WARMUP_TERM.

registerDeliveredWarmUps()
runWarmUpEvaluation()
generateWarmUpReport()
installRegistrationTrigger_()
validateWarmUpEvaluation()

SCRIPT 29 — NEW

##### PacingGuideManager

Central Ledger · Import once · Runtime lookup for S23 + S24 · 8 functions

Imports the 20-unit pacing guide JSON into the PacingGuide tab. Two runtime functions exported: resolveUnitForDate_() maps any lesson date to its pacing guide unit (Script 23 uses this for shadow matrix unit assignment), and getWarmUpAnchor_() returns the teacher-authored seed prompt for that unit and course (Script 24 embeds this in every WarmUpQueue snapshot). Caches pacing guide data in Script Properties to reduce nightly Sheets API reads. Cache invalidated on re-import.

importPacingGuide()
validatePacingGuide()

getWarmUpAnchor_(dateStr, courseName)

SCRIPT 30 — NEW

##### CompetencyRubricImporter

Central Ledger · Import once · Runtime lookup for S24 · 9 functions

Imports 220 competency rubrics extracted from two Canvas Common Cartridges into the CompetencyRubrics tab. Each rubric contains a demonstration standard, 1–14 indicators, and 1–11 skill questions pre-mapped to the four warm-up archetypes via archetype_question_map. Script 24 calls addRubricsToSnapshot_() to embed this in every WarmUpQueue snapshot — giving Flow 3 structured skill questions per competency regardless of how minimal the teacher's lesson context submission is. Resolves weak point 2: sparse context is no longer a failure mode.

importCompetencyRubrics()
validateRubricImport()
getRubricForCompetency_(compId)
getRubricsForLesson_(compIds)

SCRIPT 31 — NEW

##### ArtifactCompetencyBridge

Central Ledger · 3:05am nightly · Called after S23 · 5 functions

Connects Module 1 work artifacts to Module 2 competency tracking. When a student completes an assignment (STAGING_PIPELINE status = COMPLETE), this script reads the competency IDs tagged on that assignment's Teacher Matrix row and adds them to that student's competencies_addressed in StudentProfiles. Runs at 3:05am — after Script 23's class-level baseline pass at 3:00am and before Script 25's evaluation pass at 3:15am. Strictly additive — never removes class-level coverage. The artifact-based evidence model: completion of work = demonstrated competency exposure. Stronger than attendance because a present-but-disengaged student has no artifact; a student who completed makeup work gets credit.

syncArtifactCompetencies()
addCompetencyIdsColumn_()
installArtifactSyncTrigger_()
validateArtifactSync()
getStudentCompetenciesFromArtifacts_(ss, cfg, email)

#### Complete tab inventory

All tabs live on the Central Ledger spreadsheet. Eight new tabs across both builds. No existing tabs modified.

Tab | Build | Primary Writer(s) | Append-Only | Columns |

LessonContext | Lightweight | S22 | No (status updates) | 15 (14 + warm_up_generated) |

CompetencyRegistry | Lightweight | S22b / Manual | No (active flag) | 7 |

PacingGuide | Full | S29 (import) | No (read-only) | 16 |

CompetencyRubrics | Full | S30 (import) | No (read-only) | 8 |

◈
Snapshot isolation in WarmUpQueue. Script 24 embeds the full lesson context and student profile as JSON into each queue row at 3:30am. Studio Flow 3 reads only from the queue row — never from LessonContext or StudentProfiles directly. Late profile changes or lesson edits after 3:30am have no effect on that night's generation. The snapshot is the contract between pipeline stages.

◈
No hardcoded Drive IDs anywhere. The lesson context snapshot embeds admin_root_folder_id, teacher_name, and teacher_email sourced from Script Properties via getConfig_() at queue-build time. Flow 3 resolves the warm-up folder path entirely from the snapshot. Each teacher's deployment uses their own properties — the same codebase works for any teacher without modification.

Setup

#### Deployment checklist

Lightweight Build — Steps 1–9
Full Warm-Up Build — Steps 10–17

1

###### Add Scripts 22, 22b, 26 to Central Ledger project

Same project as Scripts 02–06. Three new script files: 22_LessonContextHandler, 22b_CompetencyRegistryImporter, 26_CompetencyAlignmentLog.

2

###### Update Script 00 in Central Ledger AND Script 07 projects

Replace the existing tabs object with the complete version from 00_SharedConfig_M2_ADDENDUM.js Section A. Add m2Enabled key from Section B.

⚠ Already done in the current codebase — this addendum is merged into 00_SharedConfig.js's getConfig_() (the m2Enabled key is already present there). Nothing to copy for a fresh checkout.

3

###### Set Script Properties — Central Ledger project

Script Editor → Project Settings → Script Properties. Add M2_ENABLED = true. Required — Module 2 handlers are disabled by default when absent.

4

###### Create lightweight tabs — run createModule2Tabs_() from Script 22

Creates: LessonContext · CompetencyRegistry · AlignmentLog · ReportRegistry. Safe to re-run.

LessonContext — 14 cols
CompetencyRegistry — 7 cols
AlignmentLog — 9 cols
ReportRegistry — 7 cols

5

###### Import competency data — run importCompetencyRegistry() from Script 22b

Upload CompetencyRegistry.csv to teacher Drive folder. Run importer. Then run validateRegistryImport() — expect 221 rows: 108 for course 8177, 113 for course 8175.

6

###### Install lightweight trigger — run installModule2Triggers_() from Script 22

Installs runAlignmentLogBackfill_ every 5 minutes. Safe to re-run. Also logs status of all required tabs.

7

###### Update and redeploy Script 07

Replace Script 07 project contents with updated file. Manage deployments → existing deployment → Edit → New version → Deploy. URL unchanged. Teachers need no new link.

8

###### Verify Script Properties — teacher identity

Confirm TEACHER_EMAIL and TEACHER_NAME are set in the Central Ledger project's Script Properties (written by Script 16 during M1 setup). Script 22 reads these to inject teacher identity into every LessonContext row.

9

###### Smoke test — lightweight end-to-end

Open Teacher Dashboard · click "New Lesson" · confirm both course tabs load with competencies in task-number order · fill required fields · select 2–3 competencies · submit. Then: LessonContext tab → one row, status ALIGNMENT_LOGGED · AlignmentLog tab → one row per competency, competency_text populated. Run generateAlignmentReport() from Script 26 → confirm doc created · ReportRegistry row written · M2_LAST_REPORT_DOC_ID in Script Properties.

LessonContext: ALIGNMENT_LOGGED
AlignmentLog: N rows = N competencies
ReportRegistry: doc_id populated

◈
Prerequisite: Lightweight build (steps 1–9) must be complete and running before starting the warm-up build. Steps 10–17 add to an existing working installation.

10

###### Add Scripts 23, 24, 25 to Central Ledger project

Same project. Three new files: 23_StudentProfileManager, 24_WarmUpBridge, 25_WarmUpWriter.

11

###### Extend LessonContext tab — run createLessonContextWarmUpColumn_() from Script 24

Adds warm_up_generated as column 15 to the existing LessonContext tab. Safe to re-run — checks for column before adding.

12

###### Create warm-up tabs — run createWarmUpTabs_() from Script 23

Creates: StudentProfiles · WarmUpQueue · WarmUpRegistry · ClassSchedule. Safe to re-run.

StudentProfiles — 12 cols
WarmUpQueue — 19 cols
WarmUpRegistry — 13 cols
ClassSchedule — 5 cols

12b

###### Import pacing guide — run importPacingGuide() from Script 29

Upload PacingGuide_CAS_Context.json to your teacher Drive folder. Run importPacingGuide() — creates PacingGuide tab with 20 units. Run validatePacingGuide() to confirm all units have anchors. Enables warm-up anchor seeds and unit-level shadow matrix tracking.

12c

###### Import competency rubrics — run importCompetencyRubrics() from Script 30

Upload CompetencyRubrics.json to your teacher Drive folder. Run importCompetencyRubrics() — creates CompetencyRubrics tab with 220 rubrics. Run validateRubricImport() — expect 113 for 8175, 107 for 8177. Enables skill question enrichment in all warm-up snapshots.

13

###### Populate ClassSchedule tab

Manually enter one row per period. Columns: teacher_email · period · day_type · course_name · active. Day type values: DAILY (Period 1) · ODD · EVEN. Course name must match exactly the subject column in CompetencyRegistry.

12d

###### Add Script 31 — artifact competency bridge

Add 31_ArtifactCompetencyBridge.js to the Central Ledger project. Run addCompetencyIdsColumn_() to extend the Teacher Matrix with a competency_ids column. Run installArtifactSyncTrigger_() to install the 3:05am nightly trigger. Tag assignments with competency IDs in the Teacher Matrix. Run validateArtifactSync() to confirm per-student coverage is being tracked. Full cron sequence: 3:00am S23 → 3:05am S31 → 3:15am S25 → 3:30am S24.

14

###### Install warm-up triggers — run installWarmUpTriggers_() from Script 23

Installs three nightly triggers: updateAllStudentProfiles at 3:00am · runWarmUpEvaluation at 3:15am · buildWarmUpQueues at 3:30am. Safe to re-run.

15

###### Install registration trigger — run installRegistrationTrigger_() from Script 25

Installs registerDeliveredWarmUps every 5 minutes. Registers Flow 3 DELIVERED docs in WarmUpRegistry and updates LessonContext when all students in a lesson are delivered.

16

###### Configure Studio Flows 3 and 4

Flow 3 — Warm-Up Generation: Trigger: WarmUpQueue status = PENDING. Temperature: 0.7. Max tokens: 400. System prompt and archetype selection logic from CAS_Flow3_Flow4_Specification.html. Output connector creates doc in student folder (path from snapshot), shares with student, writes doc_id + status DELIVERED to queue row.

Flow 4 — Warm-Up Evaluation: Trigger: WarmUpQueue status = PENDING_EVAL. Temperature: 0.2. Max tokens: 256. Returns { grammar, engagement, feedback } JSON only. Writes grammar_score, engagement_score, flow4_feedback to queue row, sets status SCORED.

17

###### Smoke test — warm-up end-to-end

Submit a lesson context for tomorrow's date. Run buildWarmUpQueues() manually — WarmUpQueue should have one row per enrolled student, status PENDING. Run updateAllStudentProfiles() manually — StudentProfiles populated. Trigger Flow 3 — warm-up docs appear in student Drive folders, WarmUpQueue rows → DELIVERED. Write a 30+ word test response in one doc. Run runWarmUpEvaluation() manually — WarmUpQueue → SCORED, WarmUpRegistry → total_score populated, feedback written into doc. Run generateWarmUpReport() — grade report doc created, ReportRegistry row written (type: WARMUP_TERM).

WarmUpQueue: SCORED
WarmUpRegistry: total_score set
Feedback in doc
ReportRegistry: WARMUP_TERM row
